# Decisions

| 2026-11-03 | Pencil test | Fingers do nothing on the canvas (touch-action none), so the page never scrolls; the toolbar and numbers sit above it. Eraser removes whole strokes; highlighter is one see-through path | Let fingers scroll a taller page |
| 2026-11-03 | Pencil test | The page is kept out of the offline cache and out of the single-page fallback, so the iPad always loads the newest copy | Cache it like the app |

Current phase only. Older decisions are in [ARCHIVE.md](ARCHIVE.md).

- **2026-11-09 · Delete page / sidebar ✕ (owner request).** Page delete has no confirm anywhere: Trash plus an Undo toast is safer and quicker; notebooks keep a confirm (now with the page count) plus Undo. The ✕ keeps its label "Delete page" (help tip says it goes to Trash). Narrow rows (container width under 19 rem, which includes the default 260 px sidebar) show a ⋯ menu for a mouse too; + and ✕ stay on hover. New texts are in enNlm/esNlm/hiNlm (late-loaded, es and hi are machine drafts). Import in the page menu opens Save & restore rather than its own dialog.

## Phase 9 decisions

- **2026-11-08 · R8-2 Pencil offer: owner says keep.** Auto switch stays on by default and the offer shows only when Auto switch is off. (Closes the open question in HANDOFF.)
- **2026-11-08 · Today button skipped:** Home › Today already covers it (owner, 8 Nov).
- **2026-11-08 · Template chooser at start (owner-approved).** Reused the N2 new-page start list instead of a second screen. Blank page is the first row (it just closes the list); then Study, then the other templates (sidebar templates and memory templates live in the same list). New notebooks now show it on their first page too (`offerStart`). "Don't show this again" turns off the synced setting `general.newPageChooser` (default on; Settings › General). Not added: database starters (they belong to databases, not pages) and no pop-up dialog (the inline list is less intrusive; the old `TemplatePicker` dialog stays for the menus). No Dexie version.
- **2026-11-08 · Owner decision: build Phase Q (Paper page) now, in parallel with the fixes.** Design in [plans/paper-page-plan.md](plans/paper-page-plan.md); building paused by the coordinator until the owner answers its open questions (section 6). Planned choice: each block box is a hidden child page, so every block feature keeps working.

- **2026-11-06 · Owner decision: build Phase L (Sync) now, in parallel with Waves 4–5.** A one-time exception to "no skipping phases". No planning talk: the approved [RESEARCH.md](RESEARCH.md) design is followed; choices are logged under "Decisions to review" (Phase L rows).

## Decisions to review

- **2026-11-09 · Feature links allowed for CI.** `paper -> editor` (box bar and box service use editor types and plain-text mapping) and `editor -> ai`, `editor -> prompts` (paste "Clean up with AI" reads the AI key and the editable cleanup prompt). No existing seam covers them; revisit if the editor should receive these through a prop.

- **2026-11-09 · Bring in from NotebookLM + optional page tabs.** (1) NotebookLM has no consumer API, so import is a one-time file or paste (research doc). (2) Cards are added as Card blocks on the chosen page; decks here are filters, so no deck is chosen. (3) Slide decks are kept as a file flagged `slides` (PDF drawn with pdf.js, PowerPoint pictures read from the zip); NotebookLM slides are pictures, not editable text. (4) The picture-to-mind-map rebuild is a choice in the preview, shows its cost first and uses the owner's key and the notebook's AI permission. (5) The three NotebookLM prompts are ordinary prompts with a new Purpose "Import" (an older library gets the option added on open); kind stays "prompt" because "paste-in" is not a kind in the library. (6) Optional tabs (Mind map, Slides, Audio & video, Database) are off by default, kept on the notebook (`pageTabs`), set from the ⋯ on the tab row or Settings › General › Page tabs, with a device default for new notebooks. (7) Database tab reuses the Quick access database view, default "this page", widened by chips. (8) Not built: drop or paste directly onto a page, a page filter on the notebook's Database tab. (9) Import is in Data › More, not a new top-level menu.

- **2026-11-09 · Outline options.** (1) A toggle line sits one level under the nearest heading above it (not under a heading hidden by the depth setting: it uses the deepest shown level), nested toggles one level deeper. (2) Chapter numbers count headings only, restart under each parent, and a skipped level (1 then 1.1.1) numbers as 1.2.1-style counters from 1. (3) Folds are not remembered, only the three view options. (4) Strings are English only for now (Hindi and Spanish fall back).

- **2026-11-09 · Notion export import.** (1) Notion has no Markdown spec for its zip, so the format is read from the known shape (names end in a 32-character id, a CSV sits beside a folder of row pages); I had no real export, only a hand-made one: owner to try a real zip. (2) A zip inside the zip, relations, formulas, comments, icons and covers are not imported. (3) Rows are matched to their .md by title. (4) The whole zip is read into memory with size caps (400 MB zip, 800 MB expanded) instead of a true stream, because the unzip library can skip big entries before inflating them; larger exports should be split in Notion.

- **2026-11-09 · Paste pipeline and chooser.** (1) Default Settings › General › Paste is "Ask each time" (the owner asked for the GoodNotes-style pop-up); it only appears for rich pastes (headings, lists, tables, bold, links, Markdown, trees) and never for our own copies or plain lines. (2) The chooser opens on Keep original format; the mouse only moves the highlight when it moves, so a resting pointer never picks for you. (3) "Always use this" remembers Keep or Plain only (As table is never remembered). (4) The chip swaps by Undo + a new paste, so one Undo still returns to before the paste. (5) Gemini's real clipboard HTML was not available: fixtures are rebuilt from its known shapes; flat HTML with Markdown text falls back to Markdown, which covers Gemini copies that arrive without structure. Owner to paste a real one. (6) Notion database detection looks for collection / table-view / property-icon markers, or Notion HTML around tab-separated text; a plain tab text with no HTML stays a Table. (7) Not built: "As text box" on paper pages (needs the paper builder's box model), "Clean up with AI" 4th option (needs the prompt library picker hooked to a paste; parked), PDF-text cleanup and "Make notes from a PDF page" (owner: later, IDEAS). How others do it: GoodNotes asks once and can remember; OneNote shows a Ctrl chip after pasting (Keep source / Merge / Text only); Notion pastes at once and offers "Paste as" in a small menu. We took the pop-up before (GoodNotes) plus the chip after (OneNote) and a Settings default; no UI was copied one to one.
- **2026-11-09 · Pasted addresses, placeholder bug, copy side.** (1) Lone YouTube address = Embed (default), other web address = Link card; Vimeo, Loom and direct .mp4 are NOT built (the Embed block would need a player for each; owner to confirm which matter first). "Placed top-centre of the paste" is not built: the Embed is a normal block at the cursor. (2) The placeholder bug could not be reproduced (synthetic paste into an empty page and a new paper box); likely a real-keyboard-only timing case in the paper builder's box focus. An e2e guards it; owner to retry on the live site after this deploys and send a screenshot if it stays. (3) The COPY side (every format on the clipboard at once, "Copy as…" submenu, round-trip test) is NOT built in this pass; it is a separate item, see HANDOFF.
- **2026-11-09 · Table row height.** Stored as a `rowHeights` prop on the table block (comma list of px, 0 = automatic) and drawn with CSS variables on the table, because a style on a row made the editor redraw it. Keyboard users cannot resize rows (the built-in column resize has no keyboard path either). Rows past 200 ignore heights.
- **2026-11-09 · Pasted database rows.** They become a Table block, never our Database block: copied rows carry no column types and I had no real Notion clipboard sample to find database markers or the header row (a copy of selected rows has no header; the first row stays a normal row). Whole databases come in through Import › Notion export (CSV to Database).

- **2026-11-09 · Select all steps.** Across several paper boxes the last step is a native browser selection over all boxes: Copy takes tables as tables, but Cut/Delete/Format only act on the box with the cursor (one editor per box). Safest without merging editors. prosemirror-tables added as a direct dependency (already installed through BlockNote).

- **2026-11-09 · Table handles.** Row-height resize, double-click "fit content" and touch drag of a column border are not built: the table stores column widths only and its resize plugin reads mouse and pen only, so they need a new block schema or our own resize code. Safest: pill and cursor on column borders (mouse/pen), BlockNote's grips on touch with 44 px targets. Owner to say if row height is wanted.

- **2026-11-09 · Paste from Notion.** Colour and highlight from Notion are not kept (the sanitiser allows no style or class); bold, italic, links, lists, dividers, callouts, toggles and one blank line are. Fixtures are rebuilt from Notion's export shapes, not a live clipboard; owner to paste a real Notion page to confirm.

- **2026-11-09 · C2b ink on PDF and EPUB pages.** The spec (master plan C2/C3) is clear on the idea and open on details; chosen the safest: (1) **No new table and no Dexie version.** Ink is kept in the existing `inkLayers` / `inkChunks` rows with `pageId` = the book page's id, so backup, History, Trash, sync and Delete forever already cover it. (2) **Block ids.** PDF: `pdf:<page>`; points are saved in page widths from that page's corner, so zoom and fit-width are exact (the unit is the first page's width). EPUB: `epub:<section>:<n>`, the section's place in the book and the paragraph's place among the section's leaf block elements (p, headings, li, blockquote, pre, figure, div, td, img ...), counted in document order. The spec said the CFI of the paragraph; this id is the same kind of path but cheap to compute for every paragraph on each layout and it does not change with text size, font or width. (3) **EPUB ink works in Scroll mode only** (the spec's own fallback): in Pages mode paragraphs break across columns and the screen is a horizontal slice. The Pen button there says "Drawing works in Scroll mode..." and does not switch the mode by itself; saved ink is hidden in Pages mode and comes back in Scroll. (4) An EPUB section that is not on screen has its ink drawn far off-screen, never moved onto another paragraph, and never adopted by the next paragraph (rule 7 does not apply to books: a book's blocks are not deleted). (5) **View as drawn is NOT built** (strokes keep `drawn: { w, fs }` with the text size so it can be added); Lasso's Text and Pictures filters pick nothing in a book (no text editor), only ink. (6) The reader has its own small drawing strip (Pen, Highlighter, Eraser, Lasso, six colours, Undo, Redo, Done) instead of the notes' main bar; pen settings are the same stored settings as in notes. Ink is only live while the strip is on; with it off the pad lets text selection and scrolling through. The tool the notes had is given back on Done or when leaving the reader. (7) The Highlighter's "snap to text lines" finds no words in a book, so it draws a plain highlight stroke. (8) Not done: ink in Liquid mode, in Page Flip previews, in PDF/Word export of a book page, and the layer gallery card is not in the reader (default layer only).

- **2026-11-09 · Ink rule 6 across boxes.** The spec only says "both go to the new block as one piece". Chosen: (1) the other box takes copies of the lines (no ids) and the ink first, and only then do they leave the old box, so a failed copy loses nothing; (2) Undo is two steps, one in each box (each box has its own editor and undo history; one shared step would need a new cross-editor history); (3) the drop spot is where the INK lands (centre), not the whole-width selection box; a box under that spot wins even when the first box's area is larger; (4) the lines go before or after the line nearest the drop; on bare paper the new box gets them at its top (its empty lines stay under them); (5) the ink keeps its place on its lines (shifted so it still sits on the same text). Same box-wins check now also applies to rule 5.

- **2026-11-08 · Fix #5 (Picture chooser).** (1) No "Link" (image web address) choice: pictures are never kept as web addresses (security rules), and fetching one needs the network and CORS; left out. (2) One chooser for Insert, short bar, "/image" and the empty block's Add button (BlockNote file panel replaced for images only; video/audio keep it). (3) Files uses an extension list plus image/* so iPad leans to the Files browser. (4) Camera in the chooser is a file input with capture; the old Camera tool stays. (5) Not testable here on a real iPad; owner check added.
- **2026-11-08 · Fix #4 (Drive pass).** (1) I could not test against real Google here; the cause is found from the code (see CHANGELOG). Google's token window cannot be opened silently on browsers that block windows without a tap, so a tap fallback is unavoidable: one top bar "Google Drive needs a tap to reconnect" + Reconnect Drive. (2) I did NOT turn a random tap anywhere into a Google window (surprising); only the bar button does it. (3) The bar shows whenever the quiet renewal fails, even with no pictures waiting (the spare key also needs Drive). (4) No new strings (reused existing, so hi/es need nothing). (5) The bar is temporary; fix #6 merges it into the alerts banner.

- **2026-11-08 · Fix #3 (unlock attempts).** (1) "3 free tries" = the 3rd wrong try starts the 1 min wait (then 5, 15, 60); the research draft had it one try later. (2) The passphrase box no longer accepts a recovery code: use the "Use your recovery code instead" link (counters are separate). (3) The code is not re-grouped as typed (it is read tolerantly instead). (4) "N of M" is not shown: the server does not say how many records wait, so the progress is "N so far" with a moving bar, then "All set". (5) The sign-in guide stays open until "All set". (6) "Make a new recovery code" shares the passphrase counter.

- **2026-11-08 · Fix #2 (two devices, same line).** (1) The other version lives on the block itself as `clash` (synced with it) instead of a new table: no Dexie version, no SQL, and the choice clears on every device by the normal save. (2) The bar shows in a stack above the page text with a short excerpt of the other version, not drawn beside the block (a floating bar over text managed by the editor could be wiped by it). (3) Keep mine only clears the mark (nothing to undo); Use other and Keep both are editor changes, so Undo takes the text back (the mark stays gone; the other version is in History). (4) I could not reproduce a lost convergence in the two-browser mock; the fixes target the read-then-overwrite gap in the merge and the never-retried typed-in block, both real. Please confirm on the iPad with real Realtime. (5) Backups and shared pages drop `clash`.

- **2026-11-08 · Fix #1 (sign in first, new recovery code).** (1) Removing the old code needs a server rule: new migration lets the app delete only recovery-code copies (`id like %-r-%`); the passphrase copy can never be deleted from the app. If the owner has not run it, the delete is refused: the new code still works, the old one too, and the app says "the old one could not be removed yet". Other option: overwrite in place (needs an update policy, riskier). (2) The new copy is read back from the server before any removal. (3) The first-run guide resumes after a restart while the flag `onboarding.guide` is set (and a fresh session signs in again); "Not now" ends it and leaves the person signed in but locked (the locked alert then reminds them). (4) Online check before Google: if the browser says offline, a message shows and nothing is started. (5) Settings › Sync keeps its inline flow; only the code screen is shared. (6) Hindi and Spanish are machine drafts for native review.

- **2026-11-08 · Notebooks locked alert (Level 1, owner defaults to review).** (1) "Later" is allowed and leaves a bright persistent banner ("Your notebooks are locked · Unlock") until unlocked, instead of a modal that cannot be closed. (2) The Update bar will be merged into this banner system later (one banner at a time), not now. (3) A "Make a new recovery code" button is planned in the sign-in fix, not built. (4) Enter passphrase and Use recovery code both open Settings › Sync, whose one box takes either; the pop-up never opens while that page is open. (5) The banner is fixed at the top and the app shell gets top padding (--attn-h), so nothing is covered. (6) needCode/unreadable also trigger it; Settings › Sync shows the unlock box for them too and unlocking clears the problem. (7) Other alert levels not built.

- **2026-11-08 · Testing-week fixes.** (1) Size trim keeps the first screen's texts in `en.ts` and moves only help tips and Settings descriptions (shown on demand) to the late-loaded `enO` group; other options were moving everything to lazy (raw keys could flash on first paint). New English strings for rarely seen screens belong in `enO.ts`. (2) Search jump: `revealBlock` asks `wakeBox` only when a placeholder exists on screen and the block is not found yet (retries every 400 ms), so other screens pay nothing. (3) The probe branches `diag/ink-budget-*` were deleted unused: their CI runs never started (billing), and the ink budget was fixed at the cause.

- **2026-11-08 · R8-6 Allow AI while locked.** (1) The switch shows only when the notebook's Allow AI is on (nothing to allow otherwise). (2) "Locked" = the page's own lock or its notebook's lock; locked databases and rows are not AI inputs today. (3) Turning the switch off or a page being locked mid-request is checked only at the start of a call (a streamed answer already running is not cut). (4) Calls with no page (none exist today) are not gated; the gate is the optional `where` on `callAi`, so a future AI entry point must pass it. (5) Hindi and Spanish text are machine drafts. Other option: one global switch instead of per notebook (rejected: owner asked per notebook). (6) Env: the build block on this PC is fixed (rollup now uses its WebAssembly build, see CHANGELOG).
- **2026-11-08 · R8-1 top gap and R8-2 Pencil offer.** (1) Owner correction: no fixed always-on band; a gap in the page's top margin holds the top pill and scrolling away leaves it floating; no sliding under floating chrome was built. Left, right, bottom pills float with no page padding, so they can cover text until dragged. (3) Trade-off: scrolled down in Text or Lasso mode the floating pill covers editable text (the A1 Scribble rule); chromeIsolation now checks the pill only at the top of the page. Owner check on iPad: a Pencil tap on a floating pill does not write into the text under it. (2) The Pencil offer is not asked when Auto switch is on (the default: the app already switches with an Undo toast), so it shows when Auto switch is off; the answer is a device preference `pencil.offer` (yes/no). Change the mode later in Settings › Device.
- **2026-11-08 · Paper ink rules 5, 6, 9.** (1) Rule 5: "no block under the drop" means the ink's centre lands outside its box; it then goes to the box under it, or a new box with empty lines is made there (same box as rule 4). Inside a box the line just above still takes the ink (as before). The hand-over is one Undo in the receiving box plus one in the old box (each box has its own history). (2) Rule 6: dragging a mixed selection moves its whole lines inside the same box, before or after the line nearest the drop; a drop on another box or on bare paper does not move lines across boxes yet (the lines go to the nearest place in their own box). The handles still change the ink only. Strokes are re-linked to the nearest selected line without an undo step, so one Undo puts lines and ink back. (3) Rule 9: only lines whose words are selected in full carry their ink (part of a line copies text only); the ink is remembered in the app (not in the system clipboard), so pasting into another app or another device has no ink; a paste is matched by its text. Pasted ink is its own Undo step after the text's. Cut removes the ink with its lines. Copy from the app's Copy/Cut buttons and Paste as plain text carry it too.

- **2026-11-08 · Polish 2 item 9 (paper style, size, thumbnails).** (1) Two ⋯ rows (Paper style, Paper size), each a ticked submenu, instead of one nested row. (2) The new-page start gets a Paper style row only; size is in the ⋯ menu. (3) The "default for new pages in Settings" part is not built here (needs a setting and a sync decision); owner to say if wanted. (4) Thumbnails show only when the open page has 2+ sheets; they are plain mini sheets with the page background, not a drawn preview of the content. (5) Size is stored explicitly (`A4` or `Letter`).

- **2026-11-08 · Polish 2 item 8 (paper hint).** The hint counts the first 3 pages opened on a device (browser storage, not synced, so "nothing is stored" holds for the notes); it shows only while the page is blank and has no extra boxes. The "+ box" tag shows for Text tool with mouse or Pencil hover only (touch has no hover).
- **2026-11-08 · Paper layout PDF.** (1) One page only (no sub-pages, not for notebooks); the entry hides when Include sub-pages is on. (2) Text is 12 pt in the box width in mm; ink scales with the box width and follows its line, so on a different text size it sits at the same place on its line, not necessarily the same letter (as on any width change in the app). (3) Highlighter strokes that sit behind the text in the app are drawn over it (they are see-through). (4) A box cut by a sheet edge is shown on both sheets. (5) Always white paper (Day), whatever the theme.

- **2026-11-08 · Paper on phones: new boxes.** (1) Two ways to tap: an always-visible "Add a box here" strip at the end (works with any tool, so it is findable) and a Text-tool tap in the gap under a box ("after the tapped box"; the gap is the only tap-safe empty spot in a stack). (2) Only the order matters on a phone, so the new box gets y = the box above + 6 mm (a full row apart); neighbours that would then read before it move down, locked ones stay (their order may differ from the wide view; rare). (3) Width = the page column, x = 15 mm.

- **2026-11-08 · Paper speed (polish-2 item 15).** (1) The plan's numbers (500 ms, 50 ms) were for a warm in-app open; the CI test measures a full reload on a 2-core machine, so the open budget is 4 s (median of three) and the Lasso 150 ms (median of five); measured here 1.2 s and 16 ms. (2) Placeholders are about three lines high, not the exact height (the height is not stored); the box grows when its editor is made. (3) A made editor is never removed again (safe against lost text). (4) Not done: jumping to a search hit inside a box that is still a placeholder does not wait for the editor (found later on scroll).

- **2026-11-08 · Automatic updates.** (1) Safe moment = visible, no pen or finger down, no waiting save, no Write pad draft with text, no dialog (`role="dialog"`/`aria-modal`), and either idle 5 s or at most 1.5 s after coming to the front with nothing touched (other: only idle). (2) A save that keeps failing is not "waiting": it goes to the question "some changes could not be saved" instead of blocking forever. (3) The pre-update backup uses a unique build name so it is due now; the next start therefore takes one more rolling backup (harmless, last 3 kept). (4) The question is asked once per app start ("Not now" stops auto-update until the next start; the Update bar is not shown while the setting is on). (5) Setting is a device setting under General, on by default. (6) A hidden test event `learning-notes:test-update` pretends a version was found (restart = plain reload); it can only reload the user's own page.

- **2026-11-08 · Polish pass 2 (items 1-7, 10-13).** (1) Welcome: shown only on a brand-new device (the first start, when no device mode is saved), so an update never greets an existing user; it takes the device picker's place there (other: also show it to everyone once, rejected as noisy). "Finger" maps to Touch mode and "Keyboard" to Computer mode; Skip writes only the seen flags. (2) Start here is made in the app's language at the time and is an ordinary notebook (not marked as a sample, so "Remove sample notebook" leaves it); its texts are lazy. (3) First-use tips use their own `firstUse.*` texts and a seen list, not the HELP_LINES registry (other: registry lines, which would also need HELP-TEXTS.md regenerated). (4) The ⇄ Last tool button is a registry tool, pinned once in Pencil mode; "Eraser returns after one erase" is off by default and only counts an erase that touched something. (5) Favourites: a ★ button saves the current pen instead of "hold an option" (a hold is already used for tips and slides on the pill); press and hold on a favourite removes it with Undo. Six per tool. They are in the bar-layout sync record, no new table. The option is in the default Pen/Highlighter pill; people with a saved pill list add it in Edit toolbar. (6) The Lasso row and the text/picture selection menu now share one order; the owner's default row (Copy, Cut, Duplicate, Convert, Colour) is unchanged, so the text menu now lists Convert and Colour right after Duplicate. The mini bar's formatting buttons are not arranged by this. (7) Item 11: the sidebar Review row already showed the due count, so only the Home tile was added. (8) Item 12 covers ink alone (the mixed Lasso menu keeps the old Make card); the card is added at the end of the page. (9) Item 13: the Layers gallery always lists the starter layers, so it needed no empty state; the in-page card list has a hint but no button (a card is made in the page). (10) Five new direct links between feature folders were added to scripts/feature-links.json (ink to images and recall for the picture card; onboarding to notebooks, pages and recall for Start here); other: moving that code to a shared folder, not worth it for lazy features. Other options for all: none costing money.

- **2026-11-08 · Phase Q owner design: every page is paper with block boxes** (supersedes the separate Paper kind). Owner defaults applied: no paper-layout template save in v1; thumbnails in the left panel; journal pages and database rows unchanged; lined 8 mm, grid/dots 5 mm. Builder choices: box geometry on `Page.boxes` (mm) and `Block.boxId` on top-level blocks, no Dexie version; one editor per box with its own snapshot so a save never touches another box; unknown box = default box. Step 2: at 100 % the default box is the old column width (sheet margins extend beside it) so existing pages look and measure the same; top margin 8 mm, the sheet top reaches up behind the help line; ink at zooms other than 100 % is step 4; paper size menu and sheet thumbnails come later in the phase. Step 3: boxes are made only by a Text-tool tap outside the default box (inside it the tap stays a caret, as before); new pages still focus the title first (existing behaviour; Enter goes to the box); step 4: each box has its own ink surface; the ink bar actions (Undo on the bar, export) still talk to the default box's ink; rule 10 needs 3+ lines and strokes of the same drawing are those overlapping within 20 s; rule 7 re-links for good (not an undo step); the Pencil on empty paper only works outside the default box, which still grows to a screen while the Pen is out (old ink pages keep working); rules 6 and 9 later. Deleting a box soft-deletes its blocks after a forced History copy, with Undo.

- **2026-11-08 · R6-21 and the ipad-webkit tap specs.** (1) A tap on a toast (and its Undo/Unlock) never closes the open options pill: the toast is feedback for what the person is doing, not a tap "outside" (other: hold the page still when the pill closes, which the R6-8 anchor cannot do at scroll 0; or make the pill float always, a bigger layout change). (2) Every spec opened through `openNote` runs with the automatic device-mode switch off (a spec that tests the switch passes its own key), because the owner's iPad is in Pencil mode throughout and the switch flipping "tap twice" on mid-test, with the bars re-laid out under the next taps, is not an iPad state (other: start the specs in Pencil mode with "tap twice" off, which changes the keyboard rules the specs cover; or a per-spec key, which is how `penTools` went red when its steps grew past the 1.5 s quiet window). (3) Multi-tap checks run on fresh pages, because our selection's drag handles sit on the next screen row and a new word under them cannot be tapped (a real person taps elsewhere first); noted as a possible polish for the handles, not changed. (4) Taps are driven inside the page on its own clock (no widened timeouts).

- **2026-11-08 · Round 7 gesture watchdog (R7-1/3/4/5/6/8).** (1) One reset bus (`app/gestureReset.ts`) that every piece with a "pointer is down" memory registers with, fired on visibility both ways, pageshow, pagehide, focus and blur (other: each piece listening on its own, which is how the stuck flags happened). (2) On a reset a half-drawn stroke with 3+ points over 50 ms is kept as writing, shorter is dropped: the same rule as iPadOS's pointercancel. (3) The kept selection lasts until the next tap or key, with a 30 s ceiling (was 1.5 s); the owner's rule is that only the person collapses a selection, and the 1.5 s window demonstrably let something else through on the real iPad. (4) A new service worker taking over an already-controlled page restarts it at a safe moment, at the latest after 20 s, so an old page never runs on new files (other: show a second "restart now" bar; rejected, the owner already pressed Update). (5) "Copy tool state" is a device-only action under Settings › Advanced next to the slow-moments report, not a screen. (6) `InkSurface` waits for the editor's container without a time limit (a MutationObserver), since after an update the editor's chunk arrives over the network. Unverified on the real iPad: the exact thing that collapsed the word at 2–5 s; the dump will tell if it still happens (`ownSelection.pointerDown`, `ink.heldEditable`).
- **2026-11-08 · Write pad round 7 (R7-15..R7-26).** (1) R7-25 (owner chose option 1): live copy, keep pad. The old "Insert automatically when I pause" is removed (its pref is ignored); the option is "Show my writing on the page as I write", on by default; with it off nothing shows on the page and closing keeps the text as a saved draft. Done = insert and close; closing the pad (X or the Insert tool) makes the draft final (other: keep it as a draft only; chosen because the owner asked "closing the pad ... make it final"). Leaving the page cannot commit (the editor is gone), the draft stays saved. (2) R7-20: the Undo list holds pad, apply and page steps; page steps are detected from ProseMirror's undo depth, so ink edits made while the pad is open are not separate steps (Undo may reach them out of order; rare). The list is dropped when the pad closes. (3) R7-15/R7-19: the Eraser works on pad strokes; the Lasso is ignored in the pad rather than selecting pad strokes (the spec allowed either; a pad Lasso is a later idea). Opening the AI canvas with the Lasso chosen switches to the Pen, because the Pencil device mode starts on the Lasso and the pad would otherwise be unwritable. (4) R7-18: the pad Eraser button is the main Eraser (one shared setting); its first press sets it to Precise + Thin once (changes the main Eraser's saved setting; other: a separate pad size). (5) R7-23: the explainer shows on every Scribble to AI switch until "Don't show again" (the owner's wording "first time" and "Don't show again" conflict; other: once only). (6) R7-24: without a cursor (page never tapped) the pad writes at the end of the page; the cause on the iPad could not be reproduced here, the likely ones (draft under the pad, plugin dropped, position between blocks) are all fixed. (7) The pad's default height is 340 px (was 300) because the look rows now wrap.
- **2026-11-08 · Round 7 batch (R7-2, 7, 10-14, 17).** (1) The automatic backup is made when the DB version is older OR the build id changed (one full copy per release, 3 kept); other: only on DB change, which would leave a data-neutral release without a copy. "Storage low" is under 25 MB free (`navigator.storage.estimate`). (2) The weekly backup reminder is not removed (it is the only guard against the browser clearing its data) but asks once per 90 days. (3) The layout sync reuses the `secrets` table (kind `toolbarLayout`) to avoid a Dexie bump while other builders work; side effect: it is not in backup files (layout is only a preference). Other: a new `prefs` table. (4) The "Edit bar" panel stays as "Bar settings…" for size, position, tabs; the old per-row checkbox lists remain there too. (5) Lasso row and selection-bar rows: not moved to the new sheet; the Lasso row order is owned by `src/ink` (a parallel helper) and the mini bar has no custom list. (6) The options pill gets no scroll cue yet, only the main bar. (7) The tool-search sections show only while nothing is typed; Recommended is a fixed list (Write pad, Find, Make card, Mind map, Audio, Summary).

- **2026-11-07 · Pencil taps and Lasso words (R6-17..R6-20).** (1) Four Pencil taps select the paragraph (the owner said "may"; other: none, three taps already give the paragraph when it is one row). (2) Under "tap twice", a second tap within 450 ms at the same spot is a double tap (selects the word); the caret-placing second tap comes after a pause (other: a 300 ms window, which the owner's real-iPad timing ruled out). (3) A whole line plus part of the next in one Lasso loop is one text range, not a block plus words; a loop that also takes in a picture keeps the block path. (4) R6-20 is read as the same fault as R6-18 (the double tap selected, then lost it), not a Pen-tool double tap, which by the owner's table does nothing.

- **2026-11-07 · Phase P part 2.** Chosen: the preview of what is sent replaces the one-time privacy step for the book panel (it shows host and text each time). Story so far on EPUB cuts the current chapter by the reader's "left in chapter" estimate (1,600 characters a unit); when that is unknown the current chapter is left out (no spoilers over completeness). Book text is capped at 60,000 characters (about 15,000 tokens); Ask keeps the start, Story so far keeps the most recent reading. The send log lives on this device only (not synced, not in backups), 500 rows, this month. Streaming only for the book answers; once text has shown, no fallback key. Notebook switches moved from Settings › AI to Settings › Permissions (one place). Feature names in the log are internal ids (askBook, summary…), not translated. Later work: the learning companion and the local MCP server (IDEAS). Other: streaming everywhere, a synced log.

- **2026-11-07 · Round 6 bar fixes.** (1) R6-8: the options pill used to push the page down by its height; now the page is held still and the pill floats over the first lines (other: keep pushing, which is what the owner called the jump). The hold lasts 450 ms and ends on any touch, wheel or foreign scroll. (2) R6-10: a palm on a bar is a touch within 400 ms of a Pencil touch or 500 ms of its lift; a Pencil left "down" without events does not block a finger. "Toolbar: finger only" covers bars and pills only, not menus or dialogs, so the Pencil can still reach Settings. (3) R6-3: I could not reproduce "acts like freeform" in Chromium; the rectangle now keeps its own start and end (not the first and last of a path), is drawn tinted, and needs no 3-point path, which a quick Pencil flick could miss. Check on the iPad. (4) R6-4: iPadOS shows its edit menu for any native text selection and a web page cannot turn it off; it is removed on our own surfaces and Pencil selections (page not focused), see LIMITATIONS. (5) R6-6: the mini bar and selection menu already had a Deselect; added it to the Lasso ink menu.

- **2026-11-07 · AI model picker.** Chosen: a notebook's own key and model is used alone (no fallback), so a Private notebook never leaks to a free Gemini key; the global order is one list of models (not per provider), the first model that has a key is the default, and a refused key (401/403/429/5xx/offline/cap) is skipped together with its other models; the free Gemini badge is a label only, cost is still estimated at paid prices so the cap stays safe; the old free-text model field is gone (cards replace it); per-key cap defaults to the global cap; the notebook stores a key id (not secret, unknown on other devices, then the default is used). Model ids for Opus 5.5, Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite and 3.6 Flash follow the providers' naming and were not called live: **check with Test**. OpenAI shows only gpt-4o-mini and gpt-4.1-mini (the GPT-5 family needs `max_completion_tokens` and hidden reasoning tokens); its prices are unverified. Other: a custom model id field, a card per key.

- **2026-11-07 · Phase P part 1.** Chosen: no streaming yet (handwriting and LaTeX answers are a few words; streaming adds parser code for little gain; long Summaries could get it later). Retries: 2, on 429/5xx/no connection only. Picture cost errs high (+85 tokens each). Convert to text always shows the picture and the "only this picture" line before sending (no separate privacy step). Replace's Undo removes the text and then asks the ink history to undo the delete (if another ink change came in between, Undo only removes the text). Write pad AI: words found by stroke gaps (0.6 of the median letter height), pressure normalised within one writing, spread under 0.12 counts as even; a word-count mismatch uses the chosen weight for all words. Maths to formula lives in the Convert sheet (not a separate Lasso action). Settings › Permissions stays inside Settings › AI (the notebook list). Other: streaming everywhere, absolute pressure thresholds, a separate Permissions screen.

- **2026-11-07 · Write pad.** Chosen: Scribble in a plain textarea (no custom pointer handlers); Replace with nothing selected acts as Insert; the live page selection wins when the page has focus, else the one saved on open or when the box is tapped; line breaks become hard breaks in the same block; one `hand` style `font.weight.size` instead of three styles; Caveat has no Light so Light shows as Normal there; the pad covers the bottom of the page (no spacer); zoom is font size, not a transform. Other: a transform zoom, Replace disabled without a selection, one style per property.
- **2026-11-07 · Name it sheet (touch/Pencil).** Chosen: the sheet is used where `(any-pointer: coarse)` matches; a mouse keeps inline rename and hover icons (fewer changes for desktop users). Other: sheet for everyone. An unchanged or empty name counts as Cancel. Section tabs keep their existing Rename dialog (field made taller on touch).
- **2026-11-07 · Safe delete.** Kept the app's existing confirm dialog for ✕ and Delete (Trash with Undo is not used for notebooks/pages here); on touch Cancel has the focus. Palm guard blocks touches for 500 ms after the Pencil lifts, only on buttons inside the sidebar, the sheet and confirm dialogs (not the editor, not Home or Library lists).
- **2026-11-07 · First load budget.** The feature needed ~1 KB over the 350 KB limit; paid for by making the Home screen lazy (Home appears after a short load on first open).

Picked by Claude. Anything can be changed.

| 2026-11-07 | R6-13 · live apply, who wins           | A block being typed in (its editor text differs from its last save, or a keystroke in the last 1.5 s) is never touched: the typing wins (the newer change, as the merge rule says) and the other device's text goes to that page's History with the "changed on two devices" notice. Everything else is updated, inserted, removed or moved in place; the caret is restored by block and text offset; remote changes are not Undo steps here. A moved block with the caret in it stays where it is until the page is reopened. Own saves that come back from the server are recognised and skipped.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Apply the other text once typing pauses and keep the typing in History (words vanish mid-sentence); character-level merging (needs a CRDT, a later phase)                                                                               |

Settings › Sync shows "Live" or "checking every 10 seconds".                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Keep 10 s always; no poll at all while live                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Now once per app start, plus at set-up and unlock; the outbox middleware tracks every save while the app runs. Reviewed and left as is: the ink controller re-measures block boxes on each keystroke when the page has strokes (O(blocks) rect reads, a few ms, no loop: the overlay sits outside the observed editor element); the free bar's and the pill's root-style observers are rAF-throttled and write no root style; help tips add document listeners only while open. The mirror starts with the sync service, not with the Settings screen.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | A separate server table and connector method (more plumbing for the same secrecy); keep keys device-only (owner asked for once-everywhere)                                                                                              |

The owner chose the double-tap numbers; the TAP numbers are mine (GoodNotes documents none). The frame loads only after a tap; `frame-src https://www.youtube-nocookie.com` is the only frame allowed. YouTube refuses frames with no referrer and the site sends `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`, so the iframe alone has `referrerPolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin"` (the site's address only, only after the tap). No thumbnail is fetched (it would tell YouTube the page was opened). Play time comes from the player's own messages (best effort, no YouTube script); typing the time always works. Pictures added while a video plays are captioned "▶ m:ss"; annotation anchors `{ type: 'time' }` wait for C1. "Paste screenshot" uses the clipboard API (may ask permission; pasting into the page works too). Not built: page previews for plain links (the app fetches nothing itself).                                                                                                                                    |

Lives in `editor/mindMap/` (not `blocks/`) so no new feature-to-feature links appear. Layout is automatic, left to right (no free x/y); rearranging = drag a branch onto another (becomes its last child) or Alt+arrows. Escape keeps the typed text. Three themes (Calm, Bold, Paper). | 2026-11-05 | Step 2 · Pencil                        | FINAL rules (owner, real iPad, supersede earlier ones). Text tool: a Pencil tap on a block with words only marks it (outline, no caret, no keyboard; setting "Pencil: tap twice to start typing", Auto = on in Pencil mode); a second tap in it places the caret exactly there; an empty block or blank space places the caret at once; no double-tap word select. Lasso: a tap marks, a second tap places the caret (no keyboard); a stroke on text selects; a blank line takes no caret; double-tap selects a word, triple-tap a line, then the mini toolbar. Pen, Highlighter, Eraser, Read: the Pencil never focuses the page. The finger always scrolls and zooms; a finger tap places the caret (one tap) outside Pencil mode.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Ask the owner again; one global rule                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

- **8 Nov, fix #6 (alerts):** (a) Level 2 uses the accent colour, not red (red kept for the locked modal/banner, real data risk). (b) "Update needed" and "backups failing" are wired but "update needed" has no source yet (no version check exists); backups count failed silent backups across starts (`attn.backupFailures`). (c) Storage warning uses the browser quota estimate (>90%), checked at start only. (d) Update × now hides 7 days (was until next start). (e) Sign-in-again, clock "Details" and Switch account all open Settings > Sync.

## Owner answers 9 Nov (parked features)

- Database pages: full Notion-like (relations, rollups, formulas, Table/Board/Gallery/Calendar). Built before the prompt library, because the library is stored as a built-in database.
- Lock editing: a lock switch makes a page or notebook read-only (no typing, no ink), no passcode.
- Prompt library: prompts from the research (own + CC0), all editable, new ones can be made. Each prompt is a database row: name, subject, task, text, inputs, preferred provider and model (toggle, with a default), outputs. Chosen from a picker like the model picker. "Skills" = a saved chain of prompts that turns one transcript into a mind map, short notes, summary, database rows and review cards in one go. Cheaper and better API use: one call returning all outputs as structured data, prompt caching on the long transcript, cheap model for simple steps; uses the owner own keys.

## Owner answers 9 Nov (skills and research books)

- Skill results: preview, edit, untick, then save into an existing or new notebook (e.g. a Research book).
- Prompt extras: versions + star rating of results; cost before running in rupees and tokens. No fill-in blanks; no Copy for Claude button (owner copies by hand).
- Outputs of one run: pages (detailed notes), keyword summary, short summary, interactive mind map, database rows, review cards of several kinds, sources.
- Symbols in notes: a free open icon set + emoji picked by the AI; owner can paste own pictures (no shipped brand icons, e.g. Adobe).
- Two ways to run: in the app with own keys, or paste-in: each prompt copies itself with the app answer format, owner runs it in any AI chat and pastes the answer back (free, no API cost).
- Priority: each key point gets 1-10 stars plus a level (Must know 8-10 / Good to know 4-7 / Extra 1-3); Must know comes first and gets a picture, a mind-map node and cards.
- Sources: as many as possible, each point linked [n] to its source, Sources list at the bottom, points with no source marked unchecked; optional second-model fact check (extra cost, shown first).

## Decisions to review (copy side, 9 Nov)

- Ctrl/Cmd+C keeps BlockNote's own copy (it already writes our lossless `blocknote/html`) and then replaces `text/plain` with clean Markdown and `text/html` with our own rich HTML, only when whole blocks are copied; words inside one block stay BlockNote's, so they paste into a line.
- Button copies (Lasso, mini bar, block menu) use a one-shot copy event so the lossless format travels too; if the browser refuses, they fall back to HTML + Markdown without it.
- Pictures in the rich HTML of a plain Copy are kept only when they are web or data addresses; "Copy as… Rich text" embeds app pictures as data URLs (same as Copy page with pictures).
- Toggles are written as `<details>` in Markdown, cards as `**Q:** … / **A:** …`; database, mind map and day record blocks have no text form and are left out of the text formats.

## Decisions to review (Clean up with AI, 9 Nov)

- Offered only when AI is on and a key exists (otherwise the chooser looks as before). A rich paste of 40+ characters, or a plain multi-line paste of 200+, up to 24,000 characters; longer text is refused with "paste less" (the answer must fit in one reply of at most 8,000 tokens).
- The instructions are the library prompt "Clean up pasted text" (starter id own.cleanup, purpose Write). If the owner deletes the row the shipped text is used; editing it in the dialog saves a new version in the library.
- The model's answer is never inserted without the preview. A local check lists numbers of the original that are not in the answer, next to the AI's own "Not placed" list.
- Only the pasted text and the instructions are sent; the notebook must allow AI. No new cost cap logic: the monthly cap applies as everywhere.

## Decisions to review (Vimeo, Loom and video files, 9 Nov)

- Vimeo: `dnt=1` (do not track) and the private-link hash of unlisted videos are kept; Loom frames use `hide_owner=true`. Frames use a sandbox (scripts, same-origin, presentation, popups; no forms, no top navigation).
- Direct video files must be https and end in .mp4 or .webm; the file loads from its own host only after Play (no preload before). No CORS or download proxy.
- Timestamps and screenshots stay YouTube only (they depend on the YouTube player messages).
- CSP `frame-src` now lists three hosts (YouTube no-cookie, Vimeo player, Loom); `media-src` already allowed https.

## Decisions to review (As text box, 9 Nov)

- Offered for rich pastes only (the same ones the chooser already appears for) and never for an address or a database. The box starts 120 mm wide (or what fits) at the caret's x and just under its line; on the stacked phone layout it goes first in reading order. Its height grows with the content like any box.
- The pasted content is made by the same parsers as the normal paste, then stored as the box's blocks in one transaction with the box; Undo is the toast (removes the box with History copy via `removeBox`).
- A locked page refuses with the usual lock message and nothing is made.

## Decisions to review (database pages, 9 Nov)

- No new Dexie version or table: the database engine, relations, rollups, formulas, tags and stars (number with stars format, max up to 10) already existed; "stars 1-10" is that format, not a new type.
- "Database page" = a page holding one full-width database block (not a new page kind), so every database feature works in notes and as a page. Offered as the last row of the new-page list.
- Search: row values go into the row's existing title entry (no new entry kind), index version 5 rebuilds once; option/tag renames show in search after the row is next saved. Checkboxes and computed columns are not searched.
- Grouping groups by the cell's shown text; multi-value cells group by their joined text. Filter and sort controls stay in Table (chips) and List (sort picker); Board and Gallery use the view's saved filters/sorts.
- Windowing starts above 200 rows (Table, List); Board/Gallery keep paging ("Show more").
- Link check: NewPageStart gets an `onMakeDatabase` prop from PageView, so templates and databases do not import each other.

## Decisions to review (prompt gaps and Lock editing, 9 Nov)

- Icons: 66 Lucide icons, drawn only in the skill preview; saved text (pages, mind map, database, cards) uses the emoji of the icon because those are plain text. Unknown names are dropped. Not a full icon picker.
- Prompts on note pages: in the page ⋯ menu only (note pages have no AI button row). Insert goes to the end of the page ("below"), not at the caret.
- Reading handwriting with a saved prompt: one device preference (ai.readPrompt) shared by Convert to text and the Write pad; applies to handwriting, never to maths; no cost line there (as before).
- Lock editing uses the field editLocked and the words "Lock editing" / "Locked — tap to unlock" to stay apart from the privacy lock (locked, PIN) and from R8-6 "Allow AI while locked" (which only concerns the privacy lock). Prompt inserts into a locked page are refused; locked notebooks are not offered as skill save targets. A page under a locked notebook has no switch of its own; the bar unlocks the notebook. Tags and row properties are not locked. Deleting is refused (menu rows disabled, desktop icons show a toast, services throw). Paper boxes: add, move, resize, menu and ink-drop are off (done in PageView; src/paper untouched).
- Not run: iPad WebKit e2e for this work.

## Decisions to review (prompt library and skills, 9 Nov)

- Library = one `Database` with `kind: 'promptLibrary'`, found by workspace and kind (not a fixed id, so two devices that both create it before syncing end up with two; the oldest is used, the other stays as an ordinary database). Columns are found by `config.key`, so the owner can rename or reorder them. Seeds are added once per starter id; ids seen on this device are remembered (pref `prompts.seeded`); a deleted starter stays deleted on this device but would come back on a fresh device.
- Prompt text is stored in a Text cell (up to 20,000 characters), versions and ratings as JSON text cells (30 versions, 100 ratings kept). Subject tags are one comma-separated text cell, not Tag records (filtering and search work in memory, a database filter on tags is not offered).
- "Stars" on a prompt = average of the 1-5 ratings of its results. The owner's "stars 1-10" is used for the key points of a skill result (database column, max 10), as decided.
- Icons: the AI picks an emoji for Must-know points (default a star); no icon set was added (nothing to download, nothing to license). The mind map node carries the emoji in its text. A free open icon set was not added because emoji already satisfy "symbols in notes", cost 0 bytes and work offline; add one later if the owner wants drawn icons.
- Rupee cost uses a fixed estimate of 90 INR per US dollar (pref `ai.inrPerUsd`, no settings screen yet, no live exchange rate: that would need a network call). Tokens are the app's usual estimate (4 characters a token); cached input is counted at full price (errs high).
- Prompt caching: Claude gets the input as the first block with `cache_control: ephemeral` (the provider ignores it below its minimum length). Streaming is not used for skills (one JSON answer). Cached tokens reported by Claude are added to the input count.
- Skills are one call (the chain is: main call, then optional fact check). "Cheap model for simple steps" = a skill that only makes keywords, summary and rows uses the cheapest model; the fact check uses a different model than the main call, else the cheapest. A preferred company and model on the row (toggle) beats both.
- Skill "Rows" = one row per key point in a new database placed in the target notebook (columns Level, Stars 1-10, Topic, Sources, Checked, Icon) and shown as a database block on the overview page. Cards: Must-know points without a card get one ("Say the key point about X"). True/false and Both ways cards become ordinary question cards (Both ways = two cards).
- Where the library opens: a Prompts button in the Library header and on source pages (book header), shown even when AI is off so the paste-in path works. Note pages have no AI button row today, so the library is reached through the Library space for them (a page can still be used as the input from the book header only). Not built: a Prompts entry on ordinary note pages, Convert-to-text and Write pad do not use the picker.
- Paste-in: the Copy button is inside each run view (the owner's "no Copy for Claude button" was read as: no separate button per assistant, the one copy works for any AI chat).
- A plain prompt run from the Library header (no page) can only be copied, not added to a page (no target page).
- Deleting a prompt asks no question; it goes to Trash with an Undo toast (rules/security.md: deletes are reversible).
- Fact check sends only the key points and the source titles (not the whole input), so it is cheap and cannot show the model more than the first call did.

- **2026-11-09 · iPad fix 2: Undo/Redo pinned:** The main bar already had them pinned, so the owner most likely saw the full ribbon; pinned there too. The ribbon may now wrap its tab row by one line on iPad widths (compact limit 85 to 100 px).

- **2026-11-09 · iPad fixes 3 and 4: Lasso on a picture, selection look:** Fix 3 root cause not found. Checked: gesture reset flag (selectGesture), stale layout, filters. Not reproducible with simulated events after a reload. Note the Lasso Pictures filter is OFF by default (owner 7 Nov), so a first loop with defaults picks no picture by design; owner please confirm the Pictures filter was on. Selection colour: orange dashed token --color-select (accessible on all three themes).

- **2026-11-09 · iPad fix 5: picture action bar:** Resize button toggles the corner handles (they are on by default so the old tap-to-resize still works). Copy puts the picture on the clipboard as an image (needs the browser's permission; Cut never deletes if the copy failed).

- **2026-11-09 · iPad fix 8: ink on pictures, bounce:** Root cause of the jump (not reproducible without an iPad): the overlay is placed from measured rects and the scroll event; the iOS bounce moves the page without scroll events, so the overlay and its clip went stale. Mitigated at three points (no bounce, per-frame placement during touch, visualViewport events) - owner please confirm on the iPad. Ink drawn on a picture BEFORE this change takes its reference width from the first session after the update (so it does not jump).

- **2026-11-09 · iPad fix 9: paper zoom spring:** The e2e uses Ctrl+wheel (same zoom code as the pinch); the touch pinch itself needs the iPad.

- **2026-11-09 · iPad fix 1: picture placement preview:** The preview opens at the cursor line (or the empty picture block) and starts at the picture's size up to 60% of the page. Moving is by dropping under a line (the page is a flow of blocks, not free positions); inside paper boxes it stays in that box's page column.

- **2026-11-09 · Paper: no text across the gap between sheets:** A single block taller than a whole sheet, or one block with Shift+Enter line breaks that crosses the end of a sheet, is moved whole only if it fits one sheet; otherwise it still crosses (rare). The box frame is not yet drawn per sheet (it is the dashed outline while the caret is in the box, now spanning the gap). Owner please check.

- **2026-11-09 · iPad fix 7 (part): boxes never overlap:** Safest option for overlap: the moved box yields (pushes itself down), the rest of the page never shifts. Resizing a box wider into a neighbour is resolved the same way (it moves down).

## Decisions (Claude Skill, 9 Nov)

- One format only: the Skill writes ln-skill/1; mind map outline = point topics, database rows = points (no new fields).
- Zip built by a Vite plugin from claude-skill/learning-notes, same URL in dev and build; not in the offline cache.
- File import lives in the existing paste-in box of a skill run (Import button and drop), 2 MB limit, same strict reader.
- The card sits at the end of Settings › AI. Strings in enO (es/hi added).

## Decisions to review (boxes like GoodNotes, 9 Nov)

| Decision                                                                                                                                              | Why                                                                       | Change it if                                               |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- |
| Handles change the **width** only (height follows the text)                                                                                           | A box's height is its text; a free height would clip or pad text          | You want fixed-size boxes                                  |
| The frame is four thin strips just outside the box (the ink pad covers the inside)                                                                    | Inside, the Lasso must keep selecting ink and lines                       | The strips are hard to hit on the iPad                     |
| Bar "Font" opens the page font card; "Size" sets Title/Heading/Body for the box                                                                       | Font and size are page-wide settings today, no per-box field              | You want a font per box (needs a box field and sync check) |
| A drop pushes only the boxes it covers (cascading down), never locked boxes or the default box; the dropped box gives way to those                    | Predictable, nothing disappears                                           | You want everything below to move                          |
| "n boxes moved" Undo toast only when more than one box changed                                                                                        | A single move needs no toast                                              | You want Undo on every move                                |
| Snap guides 1.5 mm; y snaps to the line spacing unless a box edge is near                                                                             | Helps, never fights the hand                                              | Too sticky or too weak                                     |
| Empty-box repair: an empty extra box within 12 mm of the default box or another box is removed on open; empty orphan lines too when the page has text | Fixes the stacked "Enter text" bug without touching anything with content | A deliberately empty box beside another is removed         |
| Backspace/Delete in an empty box removes it; Delete with a box selected removes it (toast Undo; History copy if it had lines)                         | An empty box must always be deletable                                     | You want a confirm                                         |
| Page-wide Cut/Delete empties every box (they stay, one empty line each) and Undo restores                                                             | The boxes themselves are the layout                                       | You want the boxes removed too                             |

## Decisions to review (design system, 9 Nov night)

- Design research, system and screen plan are in docs/research/premium-design-2026-11.md, docs/design/system.md, docs/design/screens.md. Proposed rules for rules/architecture.md are at the end of system.md: need the owner's OK.
- Sepia theme: kept as a third option (safest; no one loses their theme).
- Font: Inter loaded lazily, with the system font shown first so first load stays under the size gate.
- Accent blue → violet: owner already chose violet; first chart colour changes with it.

## Decisions to review (Drive stays connected, 9 Nov)

- Instead of a separate "exchange the code" function, Supabase Auth (which already holds the client secret) does the code exchange and returns `provider_refresh_token`; one function `drive-token` (store, token, revoke) keeps it. Fewer secrets, no new redirect address. The refresh token passes through the owner's own browser once, at sign-in, and is never kept there.
- One function, not two: smaller setup. It runs with `--no-verify-jwt` and checks the signed-in owner (and the owner email) itself, so the new publishable keys cannot break it.
- Token locked with AES-GCM using a Supabase secret (`DRIVE_TOKEN_KEY`) on top of a table with RLS on, no policy and no rights for browser roles. A leaked database dump alone cannot be used.
- Consent screen only when the server holds no token (flag `sync.driveOffline`), because Google limits refresh tokens per account and client.
- The old Google token window stays as fallback while the function is missing or has nothing stored.
